- **web-a11y-auditor**: Use to run a batch web accessibility audit (WCAG 2.2 AA) over a site's routes — a full sitemap sweep or a branch-diff-scoped pass — in an isolated context, keeping the large tree-walk off the main thread. DO NOT trigger when the user asks about historical facts, general concepts, or anything answerable from project documentation alone. - **web-researcher**: Isolated internet research. Spawn when a question needs evidence from outside this repository, such as how other products or practitioners solve a problem, published guidelines, primary sources, or prior art. Deliberately has no repository access, doesn't load the project's CLAUDE.md, and cannot spawn further agents, so its findings are far less anchored to how we already do things. Returns sourced findings with every claim labelled by evidence strength. - **web-researcher**: Researches external sources and returns a sourced Korean report: library documentation and API references, version and migration notes, technology comparisons, best practices, and troubleshooting of external issues. Read-only. ([jo-minjun/dotfiles](https://github.com/jo-minjun/dotfiles/blob/HEAD/dot_claude/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Deep web research subagent for the /research skill. Investigates a single research angle by searching the web, evaluating sources, and returning a distilled structured summary with citations. Launched in parallel batches of 4-6 by the research orchestrator. &lt;example&gt; Context: The /research skill has decomposed a topic into research angles and is dispatching subagents. assistant: "Launching web-researcher to investigate authentication patterns for WebAuthn." &lt;commentary&gt; Each research angle gets its own web-researcher subagent running in the background. &lt;/commentary&gt; &lt;/example&gt; ([kelsi-andrewss/claude-multi-agent-pipeline](https://github.com/kelsi-andrewss/claude-multi-agent-pipeline/blob/HEAD/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Finds facts outside the repository for a sprint session: official docs, specs, first-party APIs. Works from primary sources, cites every claim and saves nothing. Never decides and never talks to the user. The filled prompt is the complete task. ([likeahuman-ai/development-advanced](https://github.com/likeahuman-ai/development-advanced/blob/HEAD/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Research technical topics, documentation, best practices, and package information from the web. Use before making technology decisions or when you need current information. - **web-researcher**: Runs ONE search pass of a research run for the research or quick-research skill: finds candidate source URLs with the assigned search services, in the given source languages, and returns them as JSON lines. No page text in the result. Started by the skill, which names the pass, the languages, the queries and the services. For a quick single fact the main session asks Parallel or Exa directly, not this agent. ALWAYS spawn as a plain subagent, never as a named teammate: an in-process named teammate ignores this file's mcpServers, so the agent would run without its search services. ([marmoris-x/personal-os-setup](https://github.com/marmoris-x/personal-os-setup/blob/HEAD/home/.claude/agents/web-researcher.md) · ★1)
- **web-researcher**: Research a question using the web and return a concise, cited brief — never raw dumps. Use when a task needs current external facts, documentation, market/competitor data, or citation verification before a decision. Also runnable as the executor for storm-research lenses and roast's Researcher persona. Do not use for questions answerable from the codebase alone (use the built-in Explore agent) or for writing code. - **web-reviewer**: Reviews web (HTML5) game architecture, scene flow, rendering, input, and browser-specific risks. ([MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code](https://github.com/MRCalderon3D/everything-game-dev-code/blob/HEAD/agents/web-reviewer.md) · ★110)
- **web-reviewer**: Specialized React / Next.js / TypeScript / Tailwind review — rendering, state, data fetching, accessibility, performance. Use after writing a component, a hook or a route. ([catmargiela/ownconfig](https://github.com/catmargiela/ownconfig/blob/HEAD/agents/web-reviewer.md) · ★8)

- **web-reviewer**: Reviews TS/JS, Node, HTML/CSS/template diffs read-only on source and returns a findings table: correctness, types, XSS/injection.
